declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t1005 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/asyncfile.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t1007 = alloca i32
  %t1006 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t1005, ptr %t1007, ptr null)
  %t1008 = icmp ne i32 %t1006, 0
  br i1 %t1008, label %main.async.fail.138, label %main.async.ok.138
main.async.fail.138:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.138:
  %t1009 = load i32, ptr %t1007
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t1009
}
@will.error.data.8 = private constant { ptr, i64, i32 } { ptr @.str.40, i64 8, i32 8 }
define internal { ptr, i64 } @will.error.message.8(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t1010 = getelementptr inbounds { ptr, i64, i32 }, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0
  %t1011 = load ptr, ptr %t1010
  %t1012 = getelementptr inbounds { ptr, i64, i32 }, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 1
  %t1013 = load i64, ptr %t1012
  %t1014 = insertvalue { ptr, i64 } undef, ptr %t1011, 0
  %t1015 = insertvalue { ptr, i64 } %t1014, i64 %t1013, 1
  ret { ptr, i64 } %t1015
}
define internal i32 @will.error.status.8(ptr %data) {
entry:
